ALL reported D Decisions, preserved as a PARTIAL implementation record, not accepted design changes:

1. **D-01:** Keep the integration dormant and change ONLY the two D paths; protect all six producer and852 dependency bytes. No runtime/docs/config/schema repair under D scope.
2. **D-02:** Export one coordinator and two readonly dependency/publication interfaces, preserving the exact existing three-argument prepared chunk API.
3. **D-03:** Inject actual repositories and the actual RPC request signature, not a fake persistence abstraction. Same-connection construction is a documented caller obligation rather than private-DB reflection.
4. **D-04:** Inject synchronous current publication and optional pure clock. Reuse native timers; no second lifecycle/device/alias scheduler or config option.
5. **D-05:** Pin account and generation at construction. Old instances cannot adopt a new generation; new publication/proof must match account/mode/device/generation/age.
6. **D-06:** Do not use a boolean verification flag, listDevices in send path, cached metadata or stale bootstrap phone selectors.
7. **D-07:** Generic send is a non-async preparation entry, releasing its original output frame after handing fixed chunks/context to the queue. It calls A once.
8. **D-08:** Saved generic send compares actual body/fixed chunks and skips the persisted prefix; the normal saved actor still uses exact chunk/cursor API. Never reformat saved chunks.
9. **D-09:** Require authenticated real context; no fake queue/run ID. Reject concurrently reused queue/delivery identity across purposes/targets. Unique sequential operation identity and saved retry serialization remain caller obligations.
10. **D-10:** Account FIFO owns preflight/RPC/settlement, not just the transport call. Long operations rejoin between chunks; no parallel per-account byte-producing call.
11. **D-11:** Skip another owner's lease-blocked operation when choosing a chunk, allowing current owners and distinct destinations to advance.
12. **D-12:** Keep one full-message lease across generic explicit/progress inner chunks; release on every outer/standalone return. Saved deferrals retain their lease.
13. **D-13:** Bound64 queued operations/1MiB actual retained fixed text/30s admission or rejoin. Charge entire generic arrays, not just their current chunk. Active text is separate; no whole-process memory-bound claim.
14. **D-14:** Project saved progress into only accepted/success/failure counts, preventing uncharged whole-checkpoint retention under a small chunk wait.
15. **D-15:** Wake waiter deadlines even behind a120s active request; a long active RPC must not keep unwritten waiters indefinitely.
16. **D-16:** Return actual typed capacity/deadline errors; no tool operation is silently queued or labeled successful. Saved defer/attempt scheduling is outside sender.
17. **D-17:** Implement call-scope waiter cancellation by trusted operation identity, never active transport cancellation. **Current saved permanent disposition is superseded by supervisor instruction and requires correction on resumption.**
18. **D-18:** Re-read latest saved identity/phase/chunk/cursor and full lease before reserve; use actual queue begin rather than duplicated checkpoint SQL.
19. **D-19:** Explicit/progress mark latest invocation guard before metadata and again after lookup in the reservation transaction, preserving merged payload/active-row checks.
20. **D-20:** Run C selection/fresh request/metadata validation each chunk and publication/selection check after lookup. Proof/config changes during waits cannot grant stale authorization.
21. **D-21:** Keep lookup errors unwritten, interpret remote restrictions through B without adding a send recovery fence, and persist restrictions before releasing ownership.
22. **D-22:** Use actual allowlisted send request plus actual encoder validation, authoritative ACI and canonical self/group/DM shape; no invented parser/options/media path.
23. **D-23:** Commit persistent last-start/full reservation before bytes; honor restart/rewind spacing and independent account fences; expired pauseKind alone does not block.
24. **D-24:** Map actual BUSY/BUDGET/PAUSED reservation races to safe unwritten delivery-only errors with current restriction hints, rather than permanent checkpoint failure.
25. **D-25:** Consume B Ok local counts exactly once; consume B Err cumulative counts/cursor unchanged. Keep previous complete chunks distinct from current acceptance.
26. **D-26:** Retain all restriction hints beside Ok or Err; compose existing singular settlement with account Result operations, not a plural parameter or duplicate persistence.
27. **D-27:** Skip B's recovery hint only for saved settlement, because actual TASK006 UNKNOWN settlement already records it. Explicit settlement persists it itself.
28. **D-28:** Re-read account revision under settlement but require full reservation identity including startedAt; unrelated device-check revisions do not invalidate a real owner.
29. **D-29:** Carry TASK006 post-cleanup DeliveryCancelled as a value across outer transaction, project outside, preserving purge cleanup and independent hints.
30. **D-30:** Purge errors preserve observed complete/partial/unknown acceptance and counts; never resurrect queue/body/history or forge an Ok receipt after cancellation.
31. **D-31:** Retry ONLY DATABASE writes, retaining RPC classification/receipt, with250/500/1000/2000/2000ms capped delays and initial+five attempts; no RPC/provider reconstruction.
32. **D-32:** Do not hot-spin non-DATABASE conflicts/codec/ownership errors. Post-RPC persistence failure closes admissions and reports typed drain failure while durable recovery ownership remains. **Normal preflight refusal conflicts must not become an account-poisoning workaround.**
33. **D-33:** Re-read ready state inside terminal-write retry closures instead of retaining a full checkpoint over backoff waits.

ALL20 TASK-009 A Decisions retained (worker wording):
1. Use one private-constructor class created only by `enter` on a current ordinary row; exported entry discriminant returns checkpoint separately. Persisted invocation is never live authority.
2. Reuse actual QueueRepository and synchronous same-connection Result transactions. No new repository API/schema/raw SQL/private DB access/handle. Entry rejection uses nested Result to commit classification then expose Err.
3. Inspect authoritative persisted bytes at entry, before every invocation and at unhandled catch. Trust no caller payload snapshot. Valid checkpoint routes to actor even if expired/terminal/in_flight/delivered; this component does not decide actor recovery or expiry transitions.
4. Preserve actual producer Result/error identity, with sanitized DATABASE only for unexpected transaction throws. No added logger/audit/error-cause serialization. Downstream must sanitize existing producer diagnostics rather than log retained causes.
5. Keep run ID optional until explicit trusted attachment after the actual foreground run insert. Attachment itself is metadata; repository validates ownership before sending. Never invent a background run or derive identities from model data.
6. Trusted caller allocates unique operation delivery IDs. `context` is immutable metadata, not an authority token; live callbacks MUST enter invoke. This is a caller contract, not a structural-type cryptographic boundary.
7. Explicit/progress precommit marker uses CURRENT expected marker, not a cached snapshot. Live ownership is membership in this guard's issued trusted contexts; sender may change startedAt/latest operation. Foreign/reconstructed markers conflict. Tests cover actual sender updating marker and subsequent invocation.
8. Final/background require a matching current ready checkpoint; checkpoint persistence itself bars provider replay. Actual sender beginSignalChunk owns pre-send marker/reservation/cursor updates. Do not try to mark over a saved checkpoint or duplicate sender state/lease logic.
9. Latch exact queue/normalized ACI/canonical target, independent of delivery ID/purpose/content. Require canonical destination as the actual connector does; do not invent target normalization aliases. Distinct targets/accounts and non-Signal channels retain independent gates/explanation paths.
10. Handled tool failures do not reject provider execution or terminalize queue work. Only unhandled classifyFailure owns no-provider-replay rejection. Current checkpoint wins stale catches; generic invoked provider failure becomes NonRetryableRunError, existing expected typed errors remain identical.
11. Remember failed pre-invocation writes locally. Even if durable row remains ordinary, an unhandled provider catch after that failure cannot grant fresh provider retry. Return the producer state error for local-write handling, no retry timer or hidden network callback.
12. Saved reply-level PARTIAL plus proven-unsent current cursor is not an explicit/progress latch; B owns safe cursor retry. The new regression uses actual begin/settle, not an invented SQLite state.
13. Initial implementation exposed an overlap gap: two concurrent same-live/same-target callbacks could both be admitted before PARTIAL/UNKNOWN was observed. Escalated BEFORE changing this behavior; parent explicitly APPROVED narrow synchronous exact-target admission, not undocumented010 caller serialization. Added meaningful RED before implementation. This is the sole coordinated behavioral correction beyond the initial direction.
14. Approved overlap handling retains no waiting callback/body and creates no new FIFO/timer/lease. It returns existing ACCOUNT_BUSY/NOT_SENT/current unwritten/zero counts/providerRetryable=false/delivery_only to the new call, before a second marker/callback. It is unresolved-classification admission for the SAME execution/target, NOT account-slot contention. Other targets/accounts/executions still reach the actual sender FIFO/lease unchanged.
15. checkTarget also refuses unresolved exact-target authority before final/background checkpoint creation; caller must not evade busy by saving with context and bypassing invoke. Classify/latch before releasing the active claim, including reentrancy/microtasks. Returned or thrown known NOT_SENT/Ok permits later deliberate work; PARTIAL/UNKNOWN does not.
16. Unexpected explicit/progress callback failure has unclassified acceptance. Preserve its returned/thrown identity and conservatively latch the exact target before releasing admission, rather than fabricate receipt counts or reopen uncertain dispatch. Saved callback recovery remains governed by current durable checkpoint/actor, not this explicit latch. Tests cover a synchronous unexpected throw and exact thrown NOT_SENT identity.
17. Runner finally calls close; it permanently invalidates authority and clears local sets. close does NOT cancel a submitted sender call. Its late completion cannot reopen a closed guard, and reconstructed entry still sees durable evidence. No global cache or immortal timer; invocation callbacks own their normal execution lifetime.
18. The guard deliberately does not implement provider runtime, host authentication registration, actor finalization, retry scheduler, connector selection, account policy, transport, destination leases or UI/docs. B/010/012 must consume this actual API and existing producers. No claim that current dormant guard is already wired into AgentRunner.
19. All original865 paths/old assertions remain unchanged. Newly authored draft test corrections are enumerated above (actual error message, actual saved cursor, clearer concurrency RED). No global lint fixes or unrelated formatting. Prettier was restricted to the two owned additions.
20. Pinned source fixtures are not live evidence. Existing build/help are independent baseline assertions, not authored tests. Parent owns independently reviewed combined actor/component gates and serialized fake-terminal smoke; live Signal AC15 is neither run nor claimed.

ALL20 TASK-011 A Decisions retained (worker wording):
1. Consume actual ChannelConfigSchema, not a duplicate validator. It already delegates to SignalConfigSchema, normalizes its data, and implements the personal notice restriction. This gives daemon-equivalent field/default policy without importing guessed producer behavior.
2. Parent-approved dispatch correction: unsupported extras strip, not reject. Raised before edits; parent independently confirmed canonical behavior. Store only normalized returned config. No stricter local policy or producer edit was introduced.
3. Use existing options as the narrow B seam. Added `signalConfig?: unknown` and top-level `showToolCalls?: boolean`, not new flag UX, config-file loader, command, or module. Unknown input is intentional and always validated. No fabricated config/identity/metadata defaults.
4. Retain existing throw/Promise CLI API. This task explicitly exempts existing CLI functions from a Result refactor. New Signal expected failures use the actual ConfigError class, preserving typed identity/code and excluding causes. No new shared module boundary or Signal delivery-error wrapper exists; canonical producer Result APIs remain unchanged.
5. Keep Signal enabled only after canonical validation. No placeholder fallback, permissive enabled empty config, fake account/device marker, or auto-provisioning. Public Signal placeholder helper explicitly refuses with ConfigError.
6. Keep ordinary behavior intact. Existing placeholder objects, aliases, duplicate/name/type/read/write semantics and array-creation behavior are unchanged for ordinary channels. All original assertions remain; eight complete placeholder regressions were added.
7. Preserve existing mutation ordering. Name/type then read/root then duplicate then canonical config then write. Duplicate-name errors still take precedence over missing Signal fields, and missing file takes precedence over Signal field validation.
8. Parent-approved Signal-only I/O privacy correction. Raised after observing that existing helpers embed raw YAML/filesystem causes. Parent required sanitizing Signal reads/serialization/writes without globally changing helpers. Fixed typed safe messages discard raw errors/cause/path/content; ordinary errors are rethrown unchanged.
9. Parent-approved duplicate privacy tradeoff. Signal duplicate error retains precedence and useful fixed already-exists explanation but omits dynamic name/path; ordinary duplicate message is unchanged. No information from existing Signal config is echoed.
10. Reject scalar/array Signal YAML roots before mutation. This closes the observed scalar-content TypeError leak and array-root false-success path as part of the Signal read/parse boundary. It is not a whole-config validator or unrelated ordinary CLI behavior change; both cases were behaviorally RED before the check.
11. Use real filesystem failure controls, not a fake writer implementation. Automated test chmod affects only its own disposable fixture directory and restores it in finally. 1. Split prepare (durable generation handoff, no send) from deliver (queue-ID-only current replay), matching actual Runner.run entry needs without provider runtime.
2. Reuse Pick of actual checkpoint types and concrete SignalConnector/AuditRepository, not duplicate producer/report interfaces or shadow runtime APIs.
3. Receive live guard from trusted caller; never create it around a tool/provider retry or from checkpoint metadata.
4. Use checkTarget before checkpoint and invoke around live actual sender callback; context alone never grants permission.
5. Resolve current named connector/account/binding/applicable capability authority per prepare/ready cursor. Parent confirmed immutable in-producer-wait boundary: 012 must quiesce before authority replacement; no invented revocation callback.
6. Prefer current checkpoint over stale prepare body/identity and resolver/send catches. Added actual delivered-versus-stale-resolver behavioral RED before fixing resolver-error reinspection.
7. Preserve exact original body and actual prepare chunks; inclusive 1MiB original/formatted and unchanged 3MiB codec/lifetime-derived budget delegated to actual producers; no truncation/resplitting.
8. Fix lifetime at actual preparation time +24h; never renew on repair/retry. Initial save DB failure retains same in-handler text/preparation and retries only SQL with 250ms–10s capped backoff.
9. Use existing synchronous same-connection Result transactions only; no async, observability, sessionTracker or network side effects inside SQL.
10. Prepared phase is actual committed usage/session DB observation once, not callback invocation count after rollback. Tests prove rollback to zero/null then one committed result while run stays running.
11. Delivered phase delegates actual idempotent full-body MessageRepository/run/task finalization to trusted runner callback inside existing queue.finalizeSignalDelivery. This callback is required and tested with actual DB methods, not a dummy provider/runtime function.
12. Background retains no invented run/session; task/status identity remains in original payload for caller's existing `background-task:<taskId>:<status>` message key. No new checkpoint fields/schema.
13. Resume ready cursor only; trust actual persisted advancement over return receipt; never double-begin/advance/classify sender chunks.
14. Return delivery-only deferral on same-cursor refusal instead of automatically entering another bounded producer wait. A new actual rate test exposed changed refusal state incorrectly causing immediate reentry; meaningful RED preceded narrow fix.
15. Combine zero-progress unwritten connector-unavailable error with actual prior counts; preserve reply-level PARTIAL safe cursor, not whole-body retry/latch.
16. Existing producer terminal/UNKNOWN/PARTIAL remains terminal; actor ready expiry/permanent refusal goes through actual termination and records safe audit. No blind retry or fabricated accepted-chunk history.
17. Parent-approved missing-authority disposition: unknown-owner in_flight is CONFLICT/no mutation. Only serialized actual quiesced cold recoverSignalQueue (012) may terminalize interruption UNKNOWN.
18. Parent-approved live uncertainty disposition: retain original actual connector and public DB-only retryStateWrites, preserve BOTH producer Results; never use health/count0, new owner, RPC or recovery as substitute.
19. Null is permanent DeliveryCancelled. Err is not null. Purge stops remaining chunks/local finalization forever; late actual RPC may classify account only, never recreate queue/body/history/run.
20. Existing explicit NOT_SENT remains error/not queued and sender-owned lease cleanup. Tests compose a later saved reply; unknown target refuses prepare across purpose/ID while another target remains healthy.
21. Return expected typed Result/error identities; sanitize unexpected callback exceptions instead of exposing raw diagnostic strings. Expected persist Err rolls back all local writes and remains the same Error object.
22. Delivered state bypasses resolver/network, even after TTL. Actual queue already-completed state skips finalizer. Rotation only after commit, exceptions best effort, no durable rotation/outbox guarantee.
23. Escalated frozen terminal warning/audit requirement; parent approved REQUIRED actual same-DB AuditRepository dependency instead of fake in-memory once marker or outside producer edit.
24. Use bounded namespaced SHA256(JSON tuple queue/delivery) audit ID solely for identity dedup; no authority claim.

27. Keep audit details restricted to internal queue/delivery identity plus fixed enums/counts; optional pino warning only after new audit commit; logging throws do not change state.

## Review

**Merge verdict: OK with notes — TASK023’s two-file producer only.**

No issues found.

- **P0/P1/P2 findings:** NONE.

### Correct: implementation and actual loader boundary

- `tests/fixtures/claude-agent-sdk-native-guard.ts:9–36` installs the public synchronous **load-only** Node hook. SDK metadata resolution remains outside rejection; unrelated requests receive the original `url` and `context` through `nextLoad`.
- `…native-guard.ts:18–31` decodes file URLs, normalizes paths, and checks exact adjacent SDK identity components plus separator-bounded installed metadata-root containment. Encoded identities and dot/separator spellings cannot evade those checks. Sibling packages/scopes do not match.
- Rejection is the required fixed plain Error, before `nextLoad` or SDK source evaluation. There is no SDK import, fabricated module, rejecting resolve hook, private framework patch, or general network/production policy.
- `…native-guard.ts:6–10,35–41` retains one registration on the actual worker’s process object across module-cache reloads. Installation is synchronous and eager; no teardown deregisters the hook. Its closure and handle remain retained for subsequent native loads.

These choices match the frozen Node24.15 official-contract excerpts and installed declarations at `node_modules/@types/node/module.d.ts:223–247,479–486`.

### Correct: tests exercise the real seam safely

`tests/unit/providers/claude-code-sdk-native-guard.test.ts` provides:

| Lines | Verified behavior |
|---|---|
| 16–24,52–67 | Metadata-only SDK lookup; exclusive temporary fixtures; canonical target, root boundary, regular-file and exact known-body checks before loading |
| 79–113 | Native require, separator/dot variants, and governed encoded-scope/leaf imports |
| 116–126 | Ordinary variable dynamic import carrying the **actual installed** Vitest `dist` path and traversal to an inert target |
| 129–148 | Repeated installation, real `vi.resetModules()`/reimport, unchanged registration count, and fresh post-reload worker-path denial |
| 150–164 | Metadata resolution, unrelated package boundaries, and builtins preserved |
| 74–77 | Instrumentation restored without removing the actual hook |

The F3 request reaches the installed worker’s pre-RPC branch at `node_modules/vitest/dist/chunks/execute.2pr0rHgK.js:607–621`: `/@fs/` mapping preserves the dist-containing request, the worker converts its target to a file URL, and native import follows through `vite-node/dist/client.mjs:277–281,414–421`. This is not a cloned executor, server-only probe, reflected configuration assertion, or mocked refusal.

### TASK-010 completion (Claude-only routing, 2026-11-09)

Models switched to Claude Code subagents (workers haiku, reviewers opus) after Codex access ended; config amended/re-approved by Ivo. TASK-010 finished from the retained Codex WIP by a Haiku worker, reviewed twice by Claude Opus 5.5, merged at c9040ff.

Decisions (TASK-010, verbatim from worker reports):
- [TASK-010] Kept the WIP architecture: runner registers a per-execution guard; host tools resolve it from the trusted run context, never from model-supplied fields.
- [TASK-010] Broadcast: Signal-specific binding-failure message and channel-row logging apply only when channel type is `signal`; ordinary path restored otherwise.
- [TASK-010] Empty/whitespace-only Signal final text: no Signal send, no Signal delivery finalization; empty outbound history row as other channels.
- [TASK-010] Buffered Signal text: `\n\n` inserted only when a text block follows a tool call; no mid-run flush; CLI provider path unchanged.
- [TASK-010] `signalRuntime.sealAndDrain(SignalRunGeneration)` port contract (TASK-012 must implement): called once per foreground run (idempotent), awaited before saving any Signal reply, on provider failure/timeout paths, and in the run's `finally`; never for background-notification runs. Ok = new admission for that exact generation is sealed AND every admitted continuation (incl. timeout losers, retained repair Results) has settled; Ok certifies settlement, not tool success. Err/throw = settlement not certified; the port must not close/recover/replay and must stay idempotent. Runner bounds: up to 8 attempts, 250 ms–10 s backoff, 60 s overall deadline; a call pending at deadline counts as Err. Exhaustion → `SignalGenerationDrainError` (`DRAIN_EXHAUSTED`); no Signal tool invoked → normal queue retry; invoked → terminal via guard invoked marker / classifyFailure.
- [TASK-010] `retrySignalState` bounded to 8 attempts, returns last DbError (first checkpoint write in prepareSignalReply stays unbounded per design).
- [TASK-010] Background-agent tasks have no registered Signal execution, so their Signal channel_send/broadcast fail closed with EXECUTION_UNAVAILABLE (known limitation vs AC-12).
- [TASK-010] Worker ran `npm ci` + `npm run rebuild:sqlite` in the fresh worktree; better-sqlite3 binding is not prebuilt in new worktrees (cause of an initial 168-test false failure).

Open P3s from TASK-010 review (non-blocking, for later/final review):
- agent-runner.ts ~1201: fresh-session path ignores cached drain Err; comment claims settlement.
- agent-runner.ts ~1220: drain Err after a successful run throws for non-Signal origins too → provider + non-Signal tools re-run on retry. TASK-012's sealAndDrain should resolve Ok promptly for generations that never touched Signal.
- agent-runner.ts ~1537: bounded retry exhaustion in persistSignalRunResult/failUnpreparedSignalRun can orphan a `running` run row.
- signal-tool-execution.ts ~233: broadcast stops whole account on UNKNOWN/NETWORK_FAILURE (per-target ambiguity).
- channel-broadcast.test.ts ~54: added test is vacuous vs base.

Controller note: the stale integration worktree at `.worktrees/SCOPE-signal-channel/TASK-001.wdd/integration/` (clean, at ac7736f) was removed so `wddctl merge` could use `../talon.feat-signal.wdd/integration/`.

### TASK-012 completion (2026-11-09)

Haiku worker (2 partial rounds) then Sonnet 5.5 fix worker (per-task model override approved by Ivo); Opus 5.5 reviewed 2 rounds (R1: 1 P1 + 9 P2 + 2 P3, R2: clean, 4 P3). Merged at c6047e2.

Decisions (TASK-012, from worker reports):
- [TASK-012] Saved-delivery handler passed to QueueManager.startProcessing; routes via agentRunner.run so SignalInvocationGuard.enter sends checkpoints to deliver-only (zero provider calls, real-queue test). Closures read this.agentRunner lazily, survives reload.
- [TASK-012] Bootstrap recoverWithRetry: 5 attempts, doubling backoff from 250 ms (~3.75 s); on persistent Err bootstrap fails before queue/channels/bridge are created (fail-closed; next process start retries).
- [TASK-012] Reload sequence: load config+skills (pure) → quiesceClaims (no dispatch drain; providers may finish/save, design 286-299) → stopWithResult every Signal connector with up to 3 rounds of retryStateWrites repair → stop non-Signal → apply personas/skills/MCP → unregister/registerChannels/startAll new generation → rebuild runner, snapshot config, log level → resumeClaims. Persona load failure restores old generation from old config.
- [TASK-012] Signal stop that keeps failing → reload "pending", claims stay quiesced, unref'd 30 s retry timer (RELOAD_RETRY_MS), cleared on stop. Reloads serialized via reloadChain (SIGHUP/IPC/retry).
- [TASK-012] recoverFromCrash removed from reload (its contract needs fully drained dispatch); producer drain classifies the active reservation (forced UNKNOWN at 30 s cap); crash-only orphans handled by gated bootstrap recovery.
- [TASK-012] Shared Signal repos cached once per SQLite connection (signalRepositoriesFor); inbound transaction on same ctx.db.
- [TASK-012] signalGeneration starts 1 and increments per successful replacement; each registerChannels builds a new SignalDeviceRegistry; requestDeviceRefresh routed per generation to the owning connector's requestVerificationRefresh().
- [TASK-012] HostToolsBridge.sealSignalGeneration tracks admitted channel.send/broadcast handler promises (not timeout races); gate created only on admission (Ok immediately for runs with none); gate reset when registerRunAuthentication gets a new secret; sealed run refuses all channel.send/broadcast.
- [TASK-012] Duplicate ACI: every instance sharing an ACI is rejected (spec 132-135 "rejected rather than racing"); other accounts kept. Different accounts on one socket allowed.
- [TASK-012] IPC: signal-resume handler (TASK-011 schema) never sends; status adds `signal` block (TASK-011 projection + saved counts from summarizeSavedSignalDeliveries, omitted on Err); block omitted when no Signal channel configured and nothing saved.

Docs follow-ups for TASK-015 (from TASK-012 worker): document reload serialization + pending retry behaviour (claims quiesced while a Signal stop keeps failing), duplicate-ACI rejection of all instances, the `signal` status block (channels + saved counts), bootstrap failing after bounded recovery retries.

Open P3s from TASK-012 review: bridge gate reset can drop a still-pending predecessor admission after a timed-out pre-retry drain; no try/finally after quiesceClaims in performReload; saved handler reuses agentRunner.run (dedicated adapter = defense in depth); no "reload pending/claims quiesced" indicator in status.

### TASK-014 / TASK-015 completion and socket-mode decision (2026-11-13)

- [controller] Routing deviations (Ivo-approved, recorded as wddctl notes): TASK-012 fix round and TASK-013 rework run on Sonnet 5.5 instead of configured haiku; per-task plan overrides cannot be set on in_progress tasks, so plan.json keeps model null. Reviews stay Claude Opus 5.5.
- [decision, Ivo 2026-11-13] Socket trust relaxed to owner-only: socket owned by Talon UID, is a socket, no group/other bits (0600 or 0700 accepted); parent exactly 0700 owned by UID; ancestor/symlink/inode checks unchanged. Reason: Linux creates sockets as 0777 & ~umask; `umask 077` → 0700 (refused by exact-0600 check), `umask 177` → 0600 but makes signal-cli runtime dirs untraversable. Requires design.md line 90 amendment + a follow-up code task on signal-socket-trust.ts (after TASK-013/014/015 merge). Docs (TASK-014/015) already describe the owner-only rule.
- [TASK-014] add-signal skill byte-identical in .claude and .agents; agents must never run link/register/verify/challenge submission or ask for secrets; personal order link → stop bridge → plain listDevices `(this device)` + JSON createdTimestamp; invalid Signal entry fails the whole config (config-loader.ts:157-166) and reload is refused; challenge token obtained by an operator-run Note to Self test send (CLI with bridge stopped, or JSON-RPC via socat) — not live-verified; second JSON-RPC client on the bridge socket while Talon is connected is unverified.
- [TASK-015] docs/signal-channel.md operator reference; PARTIAL with unwritten/refused current chunk defers and resumes from cursor, other partial + all unknown terminal; terminal Signal deliveries move to dead_letter, which default queue-purge excludes (use --statuses dead_letter or --all); purge cancels only runs whose item holds a Signal checkpoint and reports only deleted count.
- Final-review candidates (code gaps found by docs tasks): queue-purge does not report cancellation counts (design asked for them); triggerPrefix with leading whitespace validates but never matches; no "reload pending / claims quiesced" status indicator; pre-existing bad Claude→Codex rename in .agents talon-setup skills (out of scope).
- TASK-016 note: tests/unit/deploy/starter-bundle.test.ts runs sync-starter-skills.sh before comparing; starter-stack/.claude/skills/{add-email,talon-setup-docker} are out of sync at HEAD; TASK-013's gate will fail on starter drift until TASK-016 syncs.
